ARTICLE 2
RECITALS
2.1
Recitals
The statements set forth in the recitals above are hereby incorporated herein into the terms and 
conditions of this CRA as if fully set forth herein.
ARTICLE 3
FIRM LOAD COMMITMENT
3.1
Initial Load Commitment and Firm Load Commitment
At execution of this CRA, the Initial Load Commitment shall be established using the amount 
and type of Firm Transmission Service that Eligible Customer intends to request from PJM on 
behalf of Large Load Customer through the applicable application and study process, together 
with the proposed load ramp and anticipated Service Dates identified in Exhibit A.
Following execution of the Final PJM Agreement, the Firm Load Commitment applicable during 
each Measurement Period shall be the amount and type of Firm Transmission Service that the 
Eligible Customer takes on behalf of the Large Load during each Measurement Period, as set 
forth in Exhibit A.  This amount shall be based on the amount of load for the Large Load 
scheduled to be in service during each Measurement Period, or the amount of Firm Transmission 
Service that the Eligible Customer has contracted for on behalf of the Large Load.
Page 30

PDF page 79

If Exhibit A establishes a load ramp, consistent with the amount of Firm Transmission Service 
that the Eligible Customer has contracted for on behalf of the Large Load, each scheduled load 
level becomes part of the Firm Load Commitment on the applicable Service Date.
Except as expressly provided in this CRA, Eligible Customer’s failure to begin taking Firm 
Transmission Service to serve the Large Load Facilities, take Firm Transmission Service
corresponding to a scheduled load level, or maintain that level of Firm Transmission Service
does not reduce or defer the Firm Load Commitment or the Committed Revenue Contribution, or 
excuse Large Load Customer from any payment or Credit Support obligation under this CRA.
3.2
Post-Final PJM Agreement Update and Subsequent Modification
Within thirty (30) days after execution of the Final PJM Agreement, Transmission Owner shall 
update Exhibit A to reflect the Firm Transmission Service, load ramp, and Service Dates 
established through or consistent with the Final PJM Agreement. Transmission Owner shall also 
update the Applicable Firm Transmission Service Rate, Projected Committed Revenue 
Contributions, Projected Full Firm Transmission Service Benchmarks, Credit Support 
Requirement, the applicable schedules in Exhibits A and C, and the calculations under Exhibit B 
using the information then available.
The information reflected in the Final PJM Agreement shall control over the corresponding 
preliminary information included in Exhibit A at execution. If the Final PJM Agreement 
establishes less Firm Transmission Service than the Initial Load Commitment, the Firm Load 
Commitment shall be reduced accordingly. A reduction required by this paragraph shall not 
constitute a Partial Termination and shall not result in a Partial Termination Amount.
An update required by this Section shall become effective without further agreement of the 
Parties and shall not constitute an amendment to this CRA. Transmission Owner shall provide 
the updated Exhibits and reasonable supporting calculations to Eligible Customer and Large 
Load Customer.
After the update required by this Section, a further modification to the Firm Load Commitment 
or a Service Date shall become effective only if the Parties execute any amendment required 
under this CRA and the amendment receives any required PJM or FERC acceptance or approval. 
A customer-requested reduction remains subject to Article 9.
3.3
No Transmission Owner Obligation
Except as otherwise explicitly provided herein, nothing in this Agreement shall constitute an 
obligation on the part of Transmission Owner to provide any service to any Party or place into 
service any transmission facilities by any specific date or dates. 
ARTICLE 4
EFFECTIVE DATE AND TERM
4.1
Effective Date
Page 31

PDF page 80

This CRA will become effective on the later of : (1) the date  executed by all Parties, or (2) the 
date accepted or approved by FERC, if such acceptance or  approval is required, or (3) on such 
other date as FERC may establish in an order accepting or approving the CRA (the “Effective 
Date”).
4.2
Term
Unless terminated earlier in accordance with this CRA, the term of this CRA (the “Term”) shall
commence on the Effective Date and continue until the later of:  (i) the date that is at least ten 
(10) years after the first Service Date as specified in Exhibit A; and (ii) the date on which all 
outstanding obligations under this CRA have been satisfied. 
ARTICLE 5
REVENUE CONTRIBUTIONS AND SHORTFALL AMOUNT
5.1
Committed Revenue Contribution
For each Measurement Period during the Term, Transmission Owner  shall calculate the 
Committed Revenue Contribution in accordance with this Section and Exhibit B, using the 
applicable rates, billing determinants, and other information available in accordance with the 
PJM Tariff.  The Committed Revenue Contribution shall equal the Minimum Commitment 
Percentage multiplied by the Firm Transmission Service-related charges that would apply if 
Eligible Customer took Firm Transmission Service corresponding to the Firm Load Commitment 
applicable during the Measurement Period.
The calculation shall use:
(a) the Firm Load Commitment that applies during each Measurement Period;
(b) the Applicable Firm Transmission Service Rate and the generally applicable billing 
determinants under the PJM Tariff; and
(c) the portion of the Measurement Period during which each scheduled Firm Load 
Commitment amount and Applicable Firm Transmission Service Rate applies.
If more than one Firm Load Commitment amount or Applicable Firm Transmission Service Rate 
applies during a Measurement Period, Transmission Owner 
shall calculate separately the 
amount applicable to each portion of the Measurement Period and aggregate those amounts to 
determine the Committed Revenue Contribution for the applicable Measurement Period.
Eligible Customer shall be responsible for timely payment of charges for Firm Transmission 
Service in a given calendar year on a monthly basis in accordance with the PJM Tariff.
5.2
Actual Revenue Contribution
For each Measurement Period during the Term, Transmission Owner shall calculate the Actual 
Revenue Contribution attributable to Firm Transmission Service actually taken to serve the 
Large Load Facilities in accordance with Exhibit B.  To the extent that the Large Load Customer 
has made separate payments through a retail tariff or other retail mechanism that reflect the 
assignment of Firm Transmission Service or related costs to the Large Load Customer, such 
Page 32

PDF page 81

payments shall be taken into account in determining its Actual Revenue Contribution, insofar as 
doing so does not result in costs being shifted to other Eligible Customers taking service in the 
Transmission Owner’s Zone. To the extent that the applicable Firm Transmission Service 
methodology calculates charges using an assigned Network Service Peak Load contribution or 
another allocated billing determinant, the Actual Revenue Contribution shall be calculated using 
the billing determinant assigned to the Large Load Facilities for the applicable Measurement 
Period and the same categories of rates used to calculate the Committed Revenue Contribution.
5.3
Shortfall Amount
For each Measurement Period, the Shortfall Amount shall equal the amount, if any, by which the 
Committed Revenue Contribution exceeds the Actual Revenue Contribution in accordance with 
Exhibit B.
Large Load Customer shall pay each Shortfall Amount in accordance with Article 7.
5.4
Separate Measurement Periods
Each Measurement Period shall be calculated independently.
5.5
Commencement of Committed Revenue Contribution
The determination of the Committed Revenue Contribution and any corresponding Shortfall 
Amounts shall begin on the corresponding Service Date, as set forth in Exhibit A, regardless of 
whether: (i) Eligible Customer has begun taking the corresponding level of Firm Transmission 
Service; (ii) the amount of Firm Transmission Service taken by Eligible Customer corresponds to 
the load level scheduled to apply on that Service Date; or (iii) the Large Load Facilities are 
operating at a lower load level than the one scheduled to apply on that Service Date.
Notwithstanding the foregoing, if Eligible Customer cannot begin taking the applicable Firm 
Transmission Service on a Service Date solely because the necessary transmission facilities have 
not been  completed, and such delay was not  caused in whole or in part by Large Load 
Customer’s or Eligible Customer’s failure or lack of reasonable diligence in satisfying  its 
obligations under this CRA or under any other binding agreement, tariff, regulatory order, or 
other legal requirement, the Service Date and commencement of the corresponding Committed 
Revenue Contribution shall be extended on a day-for-day basis until the Firm Transmission 
Service becomes available.  Large Load Customer’s obligations include, without limitation (a) 
construction, testing, commissioning and readiness of any facilities being constructed by Large 
Load Customer, and (b) prompt and reasonable responses to Transmission Owner’s or 
Transmission Owners’ affiliate(s)’ requests for information, approvals, or other communications 
necessary for Transmission Owner’s or Transmission Owners’ affiliate(s)’ work.
5.6  
Access to Information
Eligible Customer shall provide, or authorize access to, the service, billing, settlement, and 
allocation information from PJM or any other entity reasonably necessary for Transmission 
Owner to calculate the Actual Revenue Contribution, Shortfall Amount, or any other calculation 
Page 33

PDF page 82

required under this Agreement.
ARTICLE 6
CREDIT SUPPORT
6.1
Obligation to Post and Maintain Credit Support
In accordance with Tariff, Part VIII, Subpart N, section 441(d), Large Load Customer shall, at its 
own expense, cause Credit Support equal to the Initial Credit Support Requirement identified in 
Exhibit C to be provided in favor of Transmission Owner not later than ten (10) days after the 
end of the negotiation period specified in Tariff, Part VIII, Subpart N, section 441(c).
If Large Load Customer fails to provide the required Credit Support within that period, the 
consequences specified in Tariff, Part VIII, Subpart N, section 441(d) shall apply.
Within five (5) Business Days after receipt of the post-PJM recalculation required under Section 
3.2, Large Load Customer shall cause the Credit Support to be increased, reduced, amended, or 
replaced as necessary to equal the recalculated Credit Support Requirement.
The Credit Support shall secure Large Load Customer’s payment and other financial obligations 
under this CRA and shall at all times equal or exceed the Credit Support Requirement set forth in 
Exhibit C, subject to any reduction or treatment of prior draws expressly provided in this CRA.
The Credit Support Requirement shall be based on 100% of the Projected Full Firm 
Transmission Service Benchmarks for the applicable remaining Measurement Periods and shall 
not be reduced by the Minimum Commitment Percentage.
Large Load Customer shall cause the Credit Support to be maintained throughout the Term and 
thereafter until:
(a) all Shortfall Amounts and other amounts due under this CRA have been paid;
(b) the final reconciliation required under this CRA has been completed; and
(c) Transmission Owner has no remaining right to draw on the Credit Support under this 
CRA.
6.2 
Form of Credit Support 
Credit Support shall be a Letter of Credit in the form specified in Exhibit C and related 
attachments to Exhibit C.  
6.3
Replacement of Credit Support
Large Load Customer shall cause compliant replacement Credit Support to be provided within
five (5) Business Days after notice from Transmission Owner if:  (i) the issuer ceases to satisfy 
the requirements of Exhibit C; (ii) the Credit Support ceases to satisfy this Article 6; (iii) the 
Credit Support will expire or terminate before Large Load Customer’s obligation to maintain 
Credit Support ends; or (iv) the issuer gives notice of nonrenewal.
Page 34

PDF page 83

Replacement Credit Support provided under this Section shall be in an amount equal to the then-
applicable Credit Support Requirement, as  determined under Section 6.5 and Exhibit C as of the 
replacement date to reflect any completed Measurement Period that has been excluded from the 
Credit Support Requirement in accordance with Section 6.5, less the amount of any cash security 
then held by Transmission Owner and legally available to secure the same obligations.
6.4
Preservation Draw
If Large Load Customer fails to cause timely replacement Credit Support to be provided under 
Section 6.3, Transmission Owner may draw the lesser of: (i) the full amount then available under 
the existing Credit Support; and (ii) the then-applicable Credit Support Requirement, less any 
cash security then held by Transmission Owner and legally available to secure the same 
obligations. Transmission Owner may hold the proceeds as cash security for Large Load 
Customer’s obligations under this CRA.
A preservation draw does not constitute payment of an obligation unless and until Transmission 
Owner applies the proceeds to an amount due under this CRA.
Transmission Owner shall release cash security held under this Section after Large Load 
Customer causes compliant replacement Credit Support to be provided, except to the extent the 
cash has been applied to an amount due.
6.5
Adjustment of Credit Support Requirement
The Credit Support Requirement for each applicable period shall be calculated in accordance 
with Exhibit C.
Transmission Owner shall also recalculate the Credit Support Requirement following the update 
required under Section 3.2. That recalculation shall replace the Initial Credit Support 
Requirement and shall reflect the Firm Load Commitment, Service Dates, Applicable Firm 
Transmission Service Rate, Projected Full Firm Transmission Service Benchmarks, and other 
information established following the Final PJM Agreement.
Transmission Owner shall recalculate the Credit Support Requirement after completion of each 
Measurement Period and upon any other recalculation event specified in Exhibit C. The 
recalculated Credit Support Requirement shall equal the aggregate Projected Full Firm 
Transmission Service Benchmarks for the Measurement Periods remaining in the Term, 
including any incomplete portion of the applicable Measurement Period. A completed 
Measurement Period shall be excluded from the Credit Support Requirement only after: (i) the 
Committed Revenue Contribution, Actual Revenue Contribution, and any resulting Shortfall 
Amount for that Measurement Period have been finally calculated; and (ii) Large Load Customer 
has paid all amounts due for that Measurement Period.
If the Credit Support Requirement increases, Large Load Customer shall cause the Credit 
Support to be increased to the recalculated Credit Support Requirement within five (5) Business 
Days after receipt of the calculation.
Page 35

PDF page 84

If the Credit Support Requirement decreases, Transmission Owner shall authorize a 
corresponding reduction within five (5) Business Days of a request by the financial institution 
issuing the Credit Support for such authorization, provided that no payment default or other 
Event of Default exists and the remaining Credit Support, together with any cash security held by 
Transmission Owner, will equal or exceed the adjusted Credit Support Requirement.
Actual Revenue Contributions for a completed Measurement Period shall be used to determine 
the Shortfall Amount for that completed Measurement Period but shall not reduce the Projected 
Full Firm Transmission Service Benchmarks used to calculate the Credit Support Requirement 
for future Measurement Periods.
6.6
Draws for Unpaid Amounts
Except for a preservation draw under Section 6.4, Transmission Owner may draw on Credit 
Support only after:  (i) Transmission Owner has issued an invoice identifying an amount due 
under this CRA and the basis for that amount; and  (ii) Large Load Customer has failed to pay 
the invoiced amount within the payment period specified in Article 7.
A draw shall not exceed the amount due and unpaid.
Transmission Owner shall apply the proceeds of a draw to the unpaid amount for which the draw 
was made. Proceeds applied to a Shortfall Amount shall be credited in accordance with Section 
7.6, and proceeds applied to an Early Termination Amount or Partial Termination Amount shall 
be applied and credited in accordance with Section 9.12.  Large Load Customer’s payment 
default shall be cured to the extent Transmission Owner receives and applies proceeds sufficient 
to satisfy the unpaid amount.
6.7
Replenishment
Following a draw applied to satisfy an unpaid amount,  Large Load Customer shall cause the 
Credit Support to be restored to the applicable Credit Support Requirement within five (5) 
Business Days after notice from Transmission Owner.
6.8
Release of Credit Support
Transmission Owner shall release the remaining Credit Support within ninety (90) days after the 
latest of:  (i) expiration or termination of the Term; (ii) completion of all calculations, invoices, 
and reconciliations required under this CRA; (iii) payment of all undisputed amounts determined 
to be due under this CRA; or (iv) resolution of any pending dispute that could result in an 
additional amount payable by Large Load Customer .
Transmission Owner shall provide release or cancellation documentation relating to Credit 
Support to Large Load Customer, with a copy to Eligible Customer.
ARTICLE 7
Page 36

PDF page 85

INVOICING AND PAYMENT
7.1
Determination and Invoice
After the end of each Measurement Period, Transmission Owner shall calculate the Committed 
Revenue Contribution, Actual Revenue Contribution, and resulting Shortfall Amount in 
accordance with Article 5 and Exhibit B.
If a Shortfall Amount exists, Transmission Owner shall inform the other Parties and issue an 
invoice to Large Load Customer for the Shortfall Amount within sixty (60)  days after the end of 
the applicable Measurement Period.  Transmission Owner shall provide Eligible Customer with a 
copy of the invoice.
7.2
Payment
Large Load Customer shall timely pay all amounts invoiced and due under this CRA by no later 
than thirty (30) days after the date of receipt of the applicable invoice.  Any payment made after 
thirty (30) days, including any draw made by a Transmission Owner pursuant to Sections 6.6 and 
7.3 below shall include interest at the FERC rate set forth in 18 CFR Section 35.19.
7.3
Draw on Credit Support
If Large Load Customer fails to timely pay an invoiced amount, Transmission Owner may draw 
on the Credit Support for the unpaid amount in accordance with Section 6.6.
7.4
Review of Supporting Documentation
Upon Large Load Customer’s timely request, Transmission Owner and  if applicable, Eligible 
Customer, shall provide documentation reasonably necessary to enable Large Load Customer to
verify the accuracy of an invoice, subject to applicable confidentiality requirements.  For 
purposes of this section, a request shall be considered timely if made no more than five (5) 
Business Days after the date of receipt of the applicable invoice.  
Eligible Customer shall provide or authorize access to any service, billing, settlement, or 
allocation information reasonably necessary to support that review.
A request under this section shall not act to defer or extend the date on which payment is due 
under Section 7.2.
7.5
Disputed Amounts
Large Load Customer shall timely pay each invoiced amount notwithstanding any dispute, and 
Transmission Owner’s right to draw on Credit Support shall not be suspended by the dispute. If 
the final resolution determines that Large Load Customer paid, or Transmission Owner drew, 
more than the amount properly due, Transmission Owner shall promptly return the excess 
amount.  Any dispute must be made by no later than the date payment is due and conform to the 
Page 37

PDF page 86

requirements set forth in Section 15.1 below.
7.6
Crediting of Shortfall Amounts
All Shortfall Amounts collected by Transmission Owner shall be credited against the 
Transmission Revenue Requirement of the Transmission Owner for the Transmission  Zone in 
which the Eligible Customer is taking Firm Transmission Service.  Transmission Owner shall 
account for and credit all amounts received under this CRA in accordance with this Section 7.6, 
Section 9.12, as applicable, the PJM Tariff, and its applicable FERC-jurisdictional rate.  
Transmission Owner’s calculation, collection, accounting, and crediting of Shortfall Amounts 
shall be reflected in, and remain subject to review under, Transmission Owner’s applicable 
FERC-jurisdictional formula rate and related protocols.
ARTICLE 8
DEFAULT AND REMEDIES
8.1
Events of Default
Each of the following constitutes an “Event of Default” by the applicable Party:
(a) failure by Large Load Customer to cause the initial Credit Support to be timely 
provided in the amount and form required under this CRA;
(b) failure by Large Load Customer to cause to be timely provided replacement or 
additional Credit Support required under Article 6;
(c) failure by Large Load Customer to cause Credit Support to be replenished when 
required under Section 6.7;
(d) a material misrepresentation by a Party concerning the Firm Load Commitment, 
Actual Revenue Contribution, Credit Support, or another material matter under this CRA;
(e) assignment or attempted assignment of this CRA by a Party in violation of Article 10;
(f) repudiation of this CRA by a Party; 
(g) failure by Eligible Customer to take any PJM Tariff action expressly required of 
Eligible Customer under this CRA or a default by Eligible Customer under any PJM 
agreement relating to the provision of Firm Transmission Service; or
(h) failure by Eligible Customer or Large Load Customer to take an action expressly 
required under this CRA to request, pursue, or complete the applicable PJM process; or
(i) a default by Large Load Customer under any agreement with Transmission Owner or 
its Affiliates relating to the Large Load Facilities, including any failure to comply with 
any retail service tariffs, retail agreements, or other retail obligations that would prevent 
the Large Load Customer from meeting its obligations under this CRA;
(j) the bankruptcy or insolvency of Large Load Customer; or
(k) any other material breach by a Party  that remains uncured after the applicable period 
provided in Section 8.2.
8.2
Notice and Cure
Except as otherwise expressly provided in this CRA, Transmission Owner shall provide the 
Defaulting Party with written notice of an Event of Default describing the default in reasonable 
detail.
Page 38

PDF page 87

The Defaulting Party  shall have: (i) five (5) Business Days after receipt of notice to cure a 
Credit Support default; and (ii) fifteen  (15) days after receipt of notice to cure any other curable 
Event of Default.
8.3
Remedies
Following an Event of Default that has not been timely cured, the non-Defaulting Party or Parties  
may, in their sole discretion:
(a) if Transmission Owner is a non-Defaulting Party, draw on Credit Support in 
accordance with this CRA;
(b) recover any amount due and unpaid under this CRA;
(c) require specific performance of Large Load Customer’s obligation to cause Credit 
Support to be provided or maintained;
(d) exercise any right or remedy available under the PJM Tariff to the extent applicable to 
that Party;
(e) terminate this CRA only as expressly permitted under Article 9; and
(f) exercise any other right or remedy available under applicable law.
Transmission Owner shall credit all payments and Credit Support proceeds against the obligation 
for which they were received and shall not obtain duplicative recovery of the same amount.
8.4
Effect of Credit Support Draw
Receipt and application of Credit Support proceeds sufficient to satisfy an unpaid amount shall 
cure the applicable payment default to the extent of the proceeds received and applied.
ARTICLE 9
EARLY AND PARTIAL TERMINATION
9.1
Termination or Reduction by Large Load Customer; Withdrawal from PJM 
Process
Before execution of the Final PJM Agreement, Eligible Customer’s withdrawal or abandonment 
of the request for Firm Transmission Service from the applicable PJM process on behalf of Large 
Load Customer, or refusal to execute the Final PJM Agreement shall constitute an early 
termination of this CRA by Large Load Customer.  Large Load Customer shall pay the Early 
Termination Amount calculated under Section 9.7 and Exhibit D. Termination under this 
paragraph shall become effective upon the applicable withdrawal, abandonment, or refusal and 
shall not be subject to the 120-day notice period below.
After execution of the Final PJM Agreement, Large Load Customer may terminate this CRA 
before expiration of the Term by providing Transmission Owner and Eligible Customer with at 
least 120 days’ prior written notice. Notwithstanding the foregoing, termination shall not become 
effective unless and until (a) the Large Load Customer has paid in full the applicable Early 
Termination Amount or Partial Termination Amount, as determined pursuant to this CRA, or (b) 
Page 39

PDF page 88

if such amount remains unpaid, the Transmission Owner has drawn on the Credit Support and 
recovered the full amount of such unpaid Early Termination Amount or Partial Termination 
Amount.  
Large Load Customer shall pay the Early Termination Amount calculated under Section 9.7 and 
Exhibit D. 
Large Load Customer’s  obligation to pay the Early Termination Amount shall survive 
termination and remain secured by Credit Support until paid in full.
9.2
Partial Termination
Before execution of the Final PJM Agreement, a reduction by Large Load Customer or Eligible 
Customer in the amount of Firm Transmission Service requested through the PJM process shall 
constitute a Partial Termination.
Large Load Customer may permanently reduce a specified portion of the Firm Load 
Commitment by providing Transmission Owner and Eligible Customer with at least 120 days’ 
prior written notice and paying the Partial Termination Amount calculated under Section 9.8 and 
Exhibit D.
A Partial Termination shall become effective only after:  (i) PJM has approved, directed, or 
otherwise recognized the reduction to the extent required under the PJM Tariff; (ii) the Parties 
have executed an amendment to Exhibit A; and (iii) the amendment has become effective under 
applicable FERC requirements.
If the Large Load Facilities have not achieved the state of readiness necessary to take Firm 
Transmission Service corresponding to the applicable Firm Load Commitment by 120 days after 
each Service Date, such occurrence will constitute a Partial Termination.
9.3
Project Abandonment or Cessation
Each Party shall notify the other Parties within five (5) Business Days after becoming aware 
that:
(a) the Large Load Customer has determined that it will not construct or complete the 
Large Load Facilities or proceed with the applicable request for Firm Transmission 
Service;
(b) the Large Load Facilities have not achieved the state of readiness necessary to take 
Firm Transmission Service by 120 days after the initial Service Date;
(c) the Large Load Customer intends to permanently cease operations at the Large Load 
Facilities; or
(d) Eligible Customer intends to permanently cease taking Firm Transmission Service to 
serve the Large Load Facilities.
Upon notice or occurrence of an event described in this Section, Transmission Owner may 
terminate this CRA upon thirty (30) days’ written notice to the other Parties, and  Large Load 
Customer  shall pay the Early Termination Amount. Large Load Customer’s obligation to pay 
Page 40

PDF page 89

the Early Termination Amount shall survive termination and remain secured by Credit Support 
until paid in full.
9.4
Termination for Default
Transmission Owner may terminate this CRA upon written notice to other Parties if a Party:
(a) commits an Event of Default that is not capable of cure; or
(b) fails to cure an Event of Default within the applicable period under Article 8.
Upon termination, Large Load Customer shall pay:
(a) all Shortfall Amounts and other amounts accrued through the termination date;
(b) the Early Termination Amount; and
(c) any other amount due under this CRA, subject to the prohibition against duplicative 
recovery.
Transmission Owner may apply Credit Support or cash security to those amounts in accordance 
with Articles 6, 7 and 9.
9.5
Termination by Mutual Agreement
The Parties may terminate this CRA by mutual written agreement, subject to applicable FERC 
requirements.
Any such agreement shall specify the treatment of the Early Termination Amount, any 
outstanding Shortfall Amounts, and the remaining Credit Support provided by Large Load 
Customer.
9.6
Termination Due to Transmission Facility-Related Delay  
If any extension of a Service Date or Committed Revenue Contribution under the second 
paragraph of Section 5.5 results in a forecasted or actual extension of [Insert number of months] 
months or more to either the first  Service Date or any subsequent Service Date, Large Load 
Customer may, at its option, either (1) terminate this CRA; or (2) accept the timing and resulting 
adjustment to the applicable Service Date(s).
If Large Load Customer elects to terminate this CRA pursuant to this Section 9.6, Large Load 
Customer shall be responsible for paying an Early Termination Amount calculated pursuant to  
Exhibit D.  However, for purposes of determining the Applicable Termination Percentage under 
Exhibit D the number of days in any forecasted or actual extension to the Service Date or 
Committed Revenue Contribution shall not be counted.  
To invoke this termination right, Large Load Customer must provide written notice to 
Transmission Owner and Eligible Customer within 60 days of receipt of notice that any 
forecasted or actual extension will equal or exceed [Insert number of months] months.  
Moreover, Large Load Customer will not be permitted to invoke this termination right if the 
delay is caused in whole or part by Large Load Customer’s failure or lack of reasonable 
Page 41

PDF page 90

diligence in meeting its obligations under this Agreement or any other binding agreement, tariff, 
regulatory order or other legal requirement, as set forth in Section 5.5.
9.7
Early Termination Amount
The “Early Termination Amount” shall be calculated in accordance with Exhibit D. 
9.8
Partial Termination Amount
The “Partial Termination Amount” shall be calculated in accordance with Exhibit D.  After the 
Partial Termination becomes effective, the Committed Revenue Contribution and Credit Support 
Requirement shall be calculated using the remaining Firm Load Commitment, provided that the 
Credit Support Requirement shall continue to be based on 100% of the Projected Full Firm 
Transmission Service Benchmarks corresponding to that remaining Firm Load Commitment.
9.9
Early Termination Offset Amount
If the Early Termination Offset Amount is quantifiable when Transmission Owner issues the 
termination invoice, it shall be included as an Applicable Credit.
If the offset becomes quantifiable afterward, Transmission Owner shall reflect it in a subsequent 
reconciliation and promptly return any resulting excess payment to Large Load Customer.
Nothing in this Section requires Transmission Owner to market the Point of Delivery, solicit 
replacement load, modify its transmission planning or service processes, or enter into a service 
arrangement with another Person.
9.10
Invoice and Payment
Transmission Owner shall promptly invoice  Large Load Customer for the Early Termination 
Amount or Partial Termination Amount and provide reasonable substantiation of the calculation. 
For the avoidance of doubt, in the event that the Large Load Customer fails to timely pay an 
invoice for the Early Termination Amount or Partial Termination Amount, the Transmission 
Owner shall be permitted to draw on the Credit Support for the unpaid amount.  
9.11
Effect of Termination
Any full termination of this CRA for any reason shall result in (i) before execution of the Final 
PJM Agreement, withdrawal or cancellation of the applicable request for Firm Transmission 
Service; and (ii) after execution of the Final PJM Agreement, cancellation of the Firm 
Transmission Service taken by Eligible Customer on behalf of Large Load Customer and the 
related Transmission Service Agreement, except where FERC has accepted a replacement or 
substitute agreement that contains terms reasonably comparable to this CRA pursuant to PJM 
Tariff, Part VIII, Section 438(b).
A Partial Termination shall result in the corresponding reduction of the request for Firm 
Transmission Service or the Firm Transmission Service, as applicable.
Page 42

PDF page 91

For avoidance of doubt, termination of this CRA and related cancellation of Firm Transmission 
Service shall not prohibit Large Load Customer from seeking Firm Transmission Service in the 
future.
9.12
Crediting of Termination Amount
If the effective date of an Early Termination or Partial Termination occurs prior to the first 
Service Date and the Large Load Customer is required to pay an Early Termination Amount or 
Partial Termination Amount, as applicable, such amount shall first be applied to the Project 
Development Costs as of the effective date of such termination in accordance with PJM Tariff, 
Part VIII, Subpart N, section 441(e).  Any remaining amounts from the applicable Termination 
Amount shall be credited to the Transmission Owner's Transmission Revenue Requirement for 
the Transmission Zone in which the Eligible Customer would have taken Transmission Service. 
For the avoidance of doubt, if Transmission Owner allocates any of the applicable Termination 
Amount to the Project Development Costs, such amount shall not be recovered in Transmission 
rates.  
 
If the effective date of an Early Termination or Partial Termination occurs on or after the first 
Service Date and the Large Load Customer is required to pay an Early Termination Amount or 
Partial Termination Amount, as applicable, such amount shall be credited to the Transmission 
Owner's Transmission Revenue Requirement for the Transmission Zone in which the Eligible 
Customer would have taken Firm Transmission Service. 
ARTICLE 10
ASSIGNMENT
10.1
Assignment by Eligible Customer
Eligible Customer may not assign or transfer this CRA, or any right or obligation arising under 
this CRA, in whole or in part, without the prior written consent of Transmission Owner and 
Large Load Customer, which consent shall not be unreasonably withheld, conditioned, or 
delayed.
Notwithstanding the foregoing, Eligible Customer may assign its rights and obligations under 
this CRA to an Affiliate without Transmission Owner’s prior written consent, if:
(a) Eligible Customer provides the other Parties with prior written notice of the proposed 
assignment;
(b) the proposed assignee is an Eligible Customer under the PJM Tariff and is authorized 
to take Firm Transmission Service on behalf of the Large Load Customer;
(c) the proposed assignee assumes in writing all obligations of Eligible Customer under 
this CRA;
(d) the assignment has received any acceptance, approval, or recognition required from 
PJM or FERC; and
(e) all disputes between Transmission Owner and existing Eligible Customer as to 
invoice outstanding have been reconciled.
Page 43

PDF page 92

An assignment by Eligible Customer shall not change Large Load Customer’s status or release, 
reduce, or otherwise modify Large Load Customer’s obligations under this CRA.
An assignment shall not become effective until all applicable requirements of this Section have 
been satisfied.
10.2
Effect of Assignment
Except as expressly provided in a written assignment agreement accepted by Transmission 
Owner and Large Load Customer, an assignment shall not release the assigning Eligible 
Customer from: (i) any breach or Event of Default occurring before the effective date of 
assignment; (ii) any obligation arising from information, service, or conduct occurring before the 
effective date of assignment but calculated or invoiced afterward; or (iii) any obligation that 
expressly survives assignment.
After the effective date of a permitted assignment, the assignee shall be treated as the Eligible 
Customer for purposes of this CRA and shall perform all obligations
assigned to Eligible 
Customer under this CRA on and after the effective date of assignment.
10.3
Assignment by Large Load Customer
Large Load Customer may not assign or transfer this CRA or any right or obligation arising 
under this CRA, in whole or in part, without the prior written consent of Transmission Owner 
and Eligible Customer, which consent shall not be unreasonably withheld, conditioned, or 
delayed.
An assignment by Large Load Customer shall not become effective unless:
(a) the proposed assignee assumes in writing all obligations of Large Load Customer 
under this CRA;
(b) compliant replacement Credit Support is provided that directly secures the proposed 
assignee’s obligations under this CRA;
(c) the assignment is documented through a written amendment or assignment executed 
by the applicable Parties;
(d) the assignment has received any acceptance, approval, or recognition required from 
PJM or FERC; and
(e) All disputes between Transmission Owner and existing Large Load Customer as to 
invoice outstanding have been reconciled.
A transfer of ownership, operation, leasehold rights, or control of the Large Load Facilities does 
not, by itself, assign this CRA or release Large Load Customer from its obligations under this 
CRA.
10.4
Assignment by Transmission Owner
Transmission Owner may assign this CRA, in whole or in part, without the other Parties’ 
consent:  (i) to a successor to all or substantially all of the transmission assets or obligations 
relevant to this CRA; (ii) in connection with a merger, consolidation, reorganization, or other 
corporate transaction; (iii) to an Affiliate that assumes Transmission Owner’s obligations under 
Page 44

PDF page 93

this CRA; or (iv) as required or authorized by the PJM Tariff, FERC, or another governmental 
authority with jurisdiction.
Transmission Owner shall provide the other Parties with written notice of the assignment.
Any other assignment by Transmission Owner requires the prior written consent of the other 
Parties, which consent shall not be unreasonably withheld, conditioned, or delayed.
ARTICLE 11
REGULATORY PROVISIONS
11.1
Regulatory Effectiveness
This CRA shall be submitted, reported, designated, posted, or filed only to the extent required by 
the PJM Tariff, a FERC order, regulation, or applicable law.
Completion of project-specific information, dates, amounts, percentages, schedules, and other 
elections expressly contemplated by the FERC-accepted form of CRA shall not constitute a 
nonconforming term or condition.
Updates to Exhibits A and C and calculations under Exhibit B required under Section 3.2 are 
expressly contemplated by this CRA and shall not constitute nonconforming terms or 
amendments, except to the extent a further filing with or acceptance by FERC is required.
11.2
Required FERC Filing
If this CRA is required to be filed with FERC under the PJM Tariff, a FERC order, or applicable 
law, Transmission Owner shall submit this CRA for acceptance under Section 205 of the Federal 
Power Act in accordance with PJM Tariff, Part VIII, Subpart N.
Transmission Owner shall take the lead in preparing and prosecuting the filing and shall use 
reasonable efforts to obtain FERC’s acceptance of this CRA.
Each Party shall reasonably cooperate in connection with the filing and refrain from taking a 
position inconsistent with the acceptance of this CRA, except as required by applicable law or 
expressly permitted under this CRA.
Each Party shall bear its own costs incurred in connection with the filing unless the Parties 
otherwise agree in writing.
11.3
Relationship to Other Tariffs and Agreements
Nothing in this CRA shall be construed to grant, provide, guarantee, or otherwise convey 
transmission service under the PJM Tariff, distribution or retail service under state-regulated 
tariffs or agreements, or any other service or rate, term or condition of such service, except as 
expressly provided herein. 
Page 45

PDF page 94

Nothing in this CRA limits any obligation under the PJM Tariff or another applicable agreement 
to pay study costs, directly assigned facility costs, Network Upgrade costs, or other costs 
associated with transmission service.
ARTICLE 12
FORCE MAJEURE
12.1
Definition and Scope of Force Majeure
“Force Majeure” means an event or circumstance that:
(a) physically and directly impacts the Large Load Facilities, new transmission facilities, 
or modifications to existing transmission facilities, and/or 
(b) prevents the Affected Party from performing its obligations under this CRA, 
which event or circumstance (i) could not have been  reasonably anticipated as of the Effective 
Date and (ii) which is not within the reasonable control of, or the result of negligence or 
misconduct by, the Affected Party, and (iii) which the Affected Party is unable to overcome or 
avoid or cause to be avoided, by the exercise of reasonable care.  Subject to meeting the 
requirements of the prior sentence, Force Majeure events shall include, but not be limited to, acts 
of God; fire; flood; earthquake; war; explosions; labor disputes; lightning; hurricanes; tornadoes; 
pandemics or epidemics; riots; or acts of terrorism that affect one or more Parties.  With respect 
to a Party’s obligation to make payments hereunder, Force Majeure will be only an event or act 
that on any day disables the banking system through which a Party makes such payments.  
Nothing herein shall be construed so as to require a Party to settle any strike or labor dispute in 
which it may be involved.  Economic hardship of any Party shall not constitute a Force Majeure 
under this CRA, even if that economic hardship is caused by an event or condition that would 
have been a Force Majeure event if it has physically and directly impacted the Large Load 
Facilities, new transmission facilities, or modifications to existing transmission facilities.
No event affecting only the Large Load Customer, the Large Load Facilities, or the amount of 
Firm Transmission Service taken to serve the Large Load Facilities shall constitute Force 
Majeure with respect to Large Load Customer’s payment or Credit Support obligations under 
this CRA.
The Party claiming Force Majeure bears the burden of establishing that the event satisfies every 
requirement of this Section.
If a Party is rendered unable, wholly or in part, by Force Majeure to carry out its obligations with 
respect to this CRA (the “Affected Party”), then the obligations of the Affected Party will, to the 
extent they are affected by such Force Majeure event, be suspended during the period of time 
that the Force Majeure event renders the Affected Party unable, wholly or in part, to carry out its 
obligations.  The Affected Party will not be liable for breach of this CRA due to a Force Majeure 
event nor shall the Affected Party be liable to the other Parties for, or on account of, any loss, 
damage, injury, or expense resulting from, or arising out of such Force Majeure event.  The 
Affected Party must give written notice and full particulars of such Force Majeure event as soon 
Page 46

PDF page 95

as practical after the occurrence of the Force Majeure event.  If a Force Majeure event continues 
for over 180 days in the aggregate during any 12 month period, the Parties will meet and in good 
faith attempt to renegotiate the terms and conditions of this CRA to address and account for the 
Force Majeure event.  If the Parties are thereafter unable to mutually agree in writing on an 
amendment to this CRA to address issues arising out of the Force Majeure event, Transmission 
Owner or Large Load Customer may terminate this CRA upon ten (10) days’ written notice to 
the other Parties, subject to Section 12.2, Article 9, and any obligations that accrued or survive 
under this CRA.
12.2
Termination Due to Force Majeure   
In the event that Large Load Customer terminates this Agreement pursuant to Section 12, the 
Large Load Customer shall remain obligated to pay the Early Termination Amount.  However, if 
the Transmission Owner terminates this Agreement pursuant to Section 12, Large Load 
Customer shall not be required to pay the Early Termination Amount.
12.3
Effect of Force Majeure
If a Party is prevented by Force Majeure from performing an obligation under this CRA, that 
obligation shall be suspended only:  (i) to the extent performance is physically impossible 
because of the Force Majeure; (ii) for the period during which performance remains physically 
impossible; and (iii) while the affected Party complies with Section 12.4.
The affected Party shall not be in breach of this CRA solely because it fails to perform an 
obligation properly suspended under this Section.
Force Majeure does not:
(a) excuse or delay an obligation to pay an amount due under this CRA, except solely to 
the extent expressly provided in Section 12.1;
(b) excuse or delay Large Load Customer’s obligation to cause Credit Support to be 
provided, maintained, increased, renewed, replaced, or replenished;
(c) except for an extension of a Service Date expressly permitted under Section 5.5, 
reduce, defer, or otherwise modify the Firm Load Commitment, any Service Date, or the 
Minimum Commitment Percentage;
(d) reduce or defer the calculation of the Committed Revenue Contribution, Actual 
Revenue Contribution, Shortfall Amount, Early Termination Amount, or Partial 
Termination Amount;
(e) excuse Eligible Customer’s failure to take Firm Transmission Service corresponding 
to the Firm Load Commitment;
(f) require Transmission Owner to release or reduce Credit Support;
(g) prevent Transmission Owner from issuing an invoice, completing a calculation or 
reconciliation, or drawing on Credit Support in accordance with this CRA; 
(h) excuse any obligation that arose before the Force Majeure began; or
(i) affect the application of Article 9 to any abandonment, permanent cessation, 
permanent reduction, or termination associated with the Force Majeure.
For the avoidance of doubt, a Force Majeure affecting the Large Load Customer, the Large Load 
Page 47

PDF page 96

Facilities, or the amount of Firm Transmission Service taken to serve the Large Load Facilities 
shall not reduce, defer, or otherwise modify Large Load Customer’s payment or Credit Support 
obligations under this CRA. If circumstances associated with a Force Majeure result in 
abandonment, permanent cessation, permanent reduction, or termination, Article 9 shall govern 
the availability, effectiveness, and financial consequences of that abandonment, cessation, 
reduction, or termination.
12.4
Notice
A Party claiming Force Majeure shall provide the other Parties with written notice as soon as 
reasonably practicable after becoming aware of the event.
Failure to provide timely notice shall not prevent an event from qualifying as Force Majeure, but 
the affected Party shall not be excused from performance to the extent the delay in notice 
materially prejudices  another Party.
ARTICLE 13
REPRESENTATIONS AND WARRANTIES
13.1
Mutual Representations and Warranties
Each Party represents and warrants to the other Parties, as of the date it executes this CRA and as 
of the Effective Date, that:
(a) it is duly organized, validly existing, and in good standing under the laws of its 
jurisdiction of organization, to the extent such concepts apply;
(b) it has the power and authority to execute, deliver, and perform this CRA;
(c) its execution by the Parties, delivery, and performance of this CRA have been duly 
authorized by all necessary organizational action;
(d) the individual executing this CRA on its behalf has authority to bind it;
(e) this CRA constitutes its legal, valid, and binding obligation, enforceable against it in 
accordance with its terms, subject to applicable bankruptcy, insolvency, reorganization, 
moratorium, and similar laws affecting creditors’ rights generally and to general 
principles of equity;
(f) its execution by the Parties, delivery, and performance of this CRA do not violate its 
organizational documents, applicable law, or any agreement binding on it in a manner 
that would materially impair its performance under this CRA; and
(g) except for any acceptance, approval, filing, or other action expressly contemplated by 
this CRA, the PJM Tariff, or applicable law, no consent or approval is required for its 
execution by the Parties, delivery, or performance of this CRA that has not been obtained 
or will not be obtained when required.
13.2
Eligible Customer Representations and Warranties
Eligible Customer represents and warrants to the other Parties, as of the date it executes this 
CRA and as of the Effective Date, that:
(a) it is an Eligible Customer under the PJM Tariff;
(b) it is authorized under the PJM Tariff and applicable law to take Firm Transmission 
Page 48

PDF page 97

Service on behalf of the Large Load Customer to serve the Large Load Facilities;
(c) it has obtained, or will obtain when required, all authority, consent, and information 
necessary to perform its obligations under this CRA;
(d) all information provided by or on behalf of Eligible Customer for purposes of 
calculating the Actual Revenue Contribution, Shortfall Amount, Credit Support 
Requirement, Early Termination Amount, or Partial Termination Amount is and shall be 
materially accurate and complete;
(e) it has not relied on any representation or warranty by Transmission Owner concerning 
whether the Large Load Facilities shall be constructed, completed, achieve commercial 
operation, operate at any load level, or remain economically viable;
(f) it will provide or authorize access to the service, billing, settlement, and allocation 
information reasonably necessary to administer this CRA;
(g) Eligible Customer’s obligations under this CRA are limited to the transmission-
service, information-sharing, cooperation, and administrative obligations expressly 
assigned to Eligible Customer and do not include Large Load Customer’s payment or 
Credit Support obligations; 
(h) Eligible Customer shall timely perform its expressly assigned obligations 
notwithstanding any dispute with Large Load Customer; 
(i) it has agreed to request and pursue the Firm Transmission Service initially identified 
in Exhibit A on behalf of Large Load Customer through the applicable PJM process; and
(j) neither the failure of the Large Load Customer to perform nor a dispute between 
Eligible Customer and the Large Load Customer will excuse Eligible Customer’s 
performance under this CRA.
13.3
Large Load Customer Representations and Warranties
Large Load Customer represents and warrants to the other Parties, as of the date it executes this 
CRA and as of the Effective Date, that:
(a) it owns, operates, leases, or otherwise controls the Large Load Facilities;
(b) the information it provides concerning the Large Load Facilities, Firm Load
Commitment, Service Dates, load ramp, construction status, anticipated operations, and 
cessation or reduction of operations, and any other information required by or necessary 
to administer this CRA is and shall be materially accurate and complete;
(c) it has not relied on any representation or warranty by Transmission Owner concerning 
whether the Large Load Facilities will be constructed, completed, achieve commercial 
operation, operate at any load level, or remain economically viable; 
(d) its payment and Credit Support obligations under this CRA are direct obligations to 
Transmission Owner and do not depend on the performance of Eligible Customer or any 
other Person; 
(e) Credit Support provided under Article 6 directly secures Large Load Customer’s 
obligations under this CRA and, to Large Load Customer’s knowledge, is valid and 
enforceable in accordance with its terms; 
(f) it has designated Eligible Customer to request and take Firm Transmission Service on 
its behalf and has obtained Eligible Customer’s agreement to participate in the applicable 
PJM process; and
Page 49

PDF page 98

(g) Large Load Customer has not created or permitted any undisclosed lien, competing 
claim, restriction, or other interest that would materially impair Transmission Owner’s 
rights in the Credit Support.
13.4
Transmission Owner Representations and Warranties
Transmission Owner represents and warrants to the other Parties, as of the date it executes this 
CRA and as of the Effective Date, that:  (i) it is a PJM Transmission Owner; (ii) it is authorized 
to enter into this CRA and receive amounts and Credit Support payable or provided under this 
CRA, subject to the PJM Tariff and applicable FERC requirements.
ARTICLE 14
CONFIDENTIALITY
14.1
Confidential Information
For purposes of this Article, “Confidential Information” means nonpublic commercial, financial, 
operational, technical, customer-specific, or proprietary information disclosed by or on behalf of 
one Party to  another Party in connection with this CRA, whether disclosed orally, electronically, 
or in writing, including nonpublic information concerning:
(a) the Large Load Customer or the Large Load Facilities;
(b) the Firm Load Commitment, Service Dates, anticipated or actual load, or operating 
status of the Large Load Facilities;
(c) Firm Transmission Service taken to serve the Large Load Facilities;
(d) the calculation of the Actual Revenue Contribution, Shortfall Amount, Credit Support 
Requirement, Early Termination Amount, or Partial Termination Amount;
(e) Credit Support, the financial condition of Large Load Customer , and communications 
with an issuer; and
(f) any invoice, supporting documentation, or other information exchanged in 
administering this CRA.
Information shall constitute Confidential Information only if:  (i) it is identified as confidential 
when disclosed; or (ii) its nature or the circumstances of disclosure would reasonably indicate 
that it is confidential.
14.2
Exclusions
Confidential Information does not include information that the receiving Party can demonstrate:  
(i) is or becomes publicly available other than through a breach of this CRA; (ii) was lawfully 
known to the receiving Party without a confidentiality restriction before disclosure by the 
disclosing Party; (iii) is lawfully received from another Person without a confidentiality 
restriction and without breach of an obligation owed to the disclosing Party; (iv) is independently 
developed by or for the receiving Party without use of or reliance on the Confidential 
Information; or (v) is approved for disclosure by the disclosing Party in writing.
14.3
Use and Protection of Confidential Information
Page 50

PDF page 99

A Party receiving Confidential Information shall:  (i) use the Confidential Information only as 
reasonably necessary to perform, administer, enforce, or exercise its rights under this CRA; (ii) 
protect the Confidential Information using at least the same degree of care that it uses to protect 
its own confidential information of a similar nature, but in no event less than reasonable care;
(iii) disclose the Confidential Information only as permitted under this Article; and (iv) promptly 
notify the disclosing Party upon becoming aware of an unauthorized use or disclosure of the 
Confidential Information.
The receiving Party may disclose Confidential Information to its Affiliates, employees, officers, 
directors, attorneys, accountants, auditors, consultants, insurers, lenders, issuers of Credit 
Support, and other representatives that have a need to know the Confidential Information and are 
subject to confidentiality obligations or professional duties of confidentiality.
14.4
Tariff and Regulatory Requirements
Confidential Information shall be protected and disclosed in accordance with:  (i) applicable 
FERC regulations and orders; (ii) applicable state law and any applicable retail-service tariff;
(iii) any protective order or confidentiality agreement binding on the Parties; and (iv) this 
Article.
14.5
Permitted Regulatory and Administrative Disclosures
A Party may disclose Confidential Information to the extent reasonably necessary:  (i) to comply 
with the PJM Tariff, a FERC order, applicable law, legal process, or a request or requirement of 
a governmental authority with jurisdiction; (ii) to prepare, submit, support, or prosecute a filing, 
report, audit, formula-rate update, rate proceeding, tariff proceeding, or other regulatory 
submission; (iii) to administer a service request, study, billing determination, settlement, 
calculation, reconciliation, or Credit Support requirement relating to this CRA; (iv) to enforce 
this CRA or resolve a dispute arising under it; (v) to provide information to PJM reasonably 
necessary for Tariff administration, study, billing, settlement, or other administrative purposes 
relating to the Firm Transmission Service or this CRA; (vi) to provide information to an 
applicable state regulatory authority to the extent reasonably necessary in connection with retail 
service, retail Credit Support, or another state-jurisdictional arrangement relating to the Large 
Load Facilities; or (vii) with the written consent of the disclosing Party.
With respect to disclosures identified in clauses (i)-(vi), the Party making the disclosure shall 
notify the other Party so that such Party may seek a protective order or such other relief as such 
Party may deem appropriate under the circumstances. The Party making the disclosure may 
disclose only that portion of the Confidential Information which such Party is required to 
disclose.
14.6
No Limitation on Required Information Sharing
Eligible Customer may not withhold information reasonably required to calculate, verify, 
invoice, reconcile, or dispute an amount under this CRA solely because the information is 
Page 51

PDF page 100

confidential.
Large Load Customer may not withhold facility, project, load-ramp, operational, Credit Support, 
or other information within its possession or control that is reasonably required to administer this 
CRA solely because the information is confidential.
Transmission Owner’s receipt or use of such information shall remain subject to this Article and 
any other applicable confidentiality requirement.
14.7
Survival
The obligations under this Article shall survive expiration or termination of this CRA for five (5)
years; provided that (i) information constituting a trade secret shall remain protected for so long
as it qualifies as a trade secret under applicable law; and (ii) information protected for a longer 
period under applicable law, the PJM Tariff, a regulatory order, or a binding confidentiality 
agreement shall remain protected for that longer period.
ARTICLE 15
DISPUTE RESOLUTION
15.1
Notice of Dispute
A Party disputing an invoice, calculation, Credit Support Requirement, or other matter arising 
under this CRA shall provide the other Parties with written notice.  The disputing Party shall 
provide available supporting information reasonably necessary for the other Parties to evaluate 
the dispute.  A notice under this Section shall not excuse or delay performance except as 
expressly provided in this CRA. Notwithstanding the foregoing and for the avoidance of doubt, 
the Large Load Customer is not entitled to dispute resolution for its breach of Section 6.1.
15.2
Good-Faith Negotiation
Within ten (10) Business Days after receipt of a notice under Section 15.1, representatives of the 
Parties with responsibility for administering this CRA shall meet or confer and attempt in good 
faith to resolve the dispute.
If a Party does not request or participate in the Dispute resolution set forth in this Article 15 
within 10 Business Days following the delivered date of the written request initiating the 
process, such Party waives any further right to this Dispute resolution process.  The Dispute 
resolution process set forth in this Article 15 shall, in any event, be concluded within 30 days 
following the delivered date of the written request initiating the process or such longer period as 
the Parties may mutually agree.  Thereafter, any Party may exercise all remedies available at law 
or in equity under this CRA, including the initiation of court proceedings.
15.3
Continuation of Performance
While a dispute remains pending:
Page 52

PDF page 101

(a) each Party shall continue performing its obligations under this CRA to the maximum 
extent practicable;
(b) Large Load Customer shall timely pay each invoiced amount in accordance with 
Section 7.2, notwithstanding the dispute;
(c) Large Load Customer shall continue to cause Credit Support to be provided, 
maintained, increased, renewed, replaced, and replenished as required under Article 6;
(d) Transmission Owner may exercise its rights with respect to Credit Support in 
accordance with Articles 6 and 7; and
(e) no Party shall take action inconsistent with a final and binding determination 
resolving the dispute.
Payment or performance during a dispute shall not waive the paying or performing Party’s 
position or right to seek return of an overpayment or other appropriate relief.
15.4
No Suspension of Credit Support Rights
The initiation or pendency of a dispute shall not:  (i) prevent Transmission Owner from making a 
preservation draw under Section 6.4; (ii) suspend Transmission Owner’s right to draw on Credit 
Support for an invoiced amount not timely paid; (iii) relieve Large Load Customer of its
obligation to cause compliant Credit Support to be maintained; or (iv) extend the expiration, 
nonrenewal, replacement, or replenishment deadline applicable to Credit Support.
If the final resolution of a dispute determines that Large Load Customer paid, or Transmission 
Owner drew, more than the amount properly due, Transmission Owner shall return the excess to 
Large Load Customer or, if applicable, restore the excess Credit Support in accordance with 
Section 6.5.
If a Party receiving a written request to initiate the dispute-resolution process does not participate 
in that process within 10 Business Days after delivery of the written request, that Party waives its 
right to require completion of the informal dispute-resolution process before another Party 
pursues available remedies.  The dispute resolution process set forth in this Article 15 shall, in 
any event, be concluded within 30 days following the delivered date of the written request 
initiating the process or such longer period as the Parties may mutually agree.  
Thereafter, any Party may exercise all remedies available at law or in equity under this CRA, 
including the initiation of court proceedings.

ARTICLE 17
GENERAL PROVISIONS
17.1
Amendment
This CRA may be amended only by a written instrument executed by authorized representatives 
of all Parties.
An amendment shall become effective only upon:  (i) acceptance or approval by FERC, if 
required; (ii) any approval or recognition required under the PJM Tariff; and (iii) satisfaction of 
any other requirement of applicable law.
An update to an Exhibit required under Section 3.2 shall not constitute an amendment for 
purposes of this Section.
17.2
Entire Agreement; Relationship to Other Agreements
This CRA and its Exhibits constitute the entire agreement between the Parties concerning the 
Firm Load Commitment and the payment, Credit Support, early-termination, and related 
administrative obligations established under this CRA.  
Page 55

PDF page 104

This CRA supersedes prior oral or written communications, negotiations, and understandings 
between the Parties concerning the subject matter of this agreement.  This CRA does not 
supersede or otherwise impact other agreements (which may include security or cost recovery 
provisions) that relate to the Large Load Facilities and that do not address matters set forth in this 
CRA related to Firm Load Commitments.
17.3
Severability
If a provision of this CRA is held invalid, unlawful, or unenforceable by FERC or another 
governmental authority or tribunal having jurisdiction, the remaining provisions shall remain in 
effect to the maximum extent permitted by law.
The Parties shall cooperate in good faith to replace the affected provision with a lawful and 
enforceable provision that preserves, to the greatest extent permitted by law, the original 
provision’s purpose and the economic protections established by this CRA.
If the affected provision cannot be replaced and its invalidity defeats the essential purpose of this 
CRA, the Parties’ rights shall be governed by the remaining enforceable provisions of this CRA 
and applicable law.
17.4
Governing Law
This CRA shall be governed by:  (i) federal law, including the Federal Power Act, and the PJM 
Tariff with respect to matters within federal jurisdiction; and (ii) the laws of the State of [State], 
without regard to its conflict-of-laws principles, with respect to matters not governed by federal 
law.
Nothing in this Section limits FERC’s jurisdiction or alters the allocation of jurisdiction between 
FERC, any state commission, and any court or other tribunal.
17.5
No Third-Party Beneficiaries
This CRA is entered into solely for the benefit of the Parties and their permitted successors and 
assigns.
Except as expressly provided in this CRA, no other Person, including PJM, another transmission 
customer, a Load Serving Entity, an issuer of Credit Support, or a governmental authority, shall 
have a right to enforce this CRA or be deemed a third-party beneficiary.
17.6
Relationship of the Parties
Nothing in this CRA creates or shall be construed to create:  (i) a partnership; (ii) a joint venture;
(iii) an agency relationship; (iv) a fiduciary relationship; or (v) an employment relationship
among any of the Parties..
Page 56

PDF page 105

17.7
Counterparts; Electronic Signatures
This CRA may be executed in any number of counterparts, each of which shall be deemed an 
original and all of which together shall constitute one instrument.
17.8
Indemnification
Eligible Customer and Large Load Customer shall, jointly and severally, to the fullest extent 
permitted by law, indemnify, defend upon request, and hold harmless Transmission Owner and 
its Representatives from and against all claims, damages, expenses, liabilities, and losses 
(including, without limitation, reasonable attorneys’ fees and expenses) asserted against, 
sustained, or incurred by Transmission Owner or its Representatives for any damage, harm, 
injury, or loss, of any kind, direct or indirect, to any property or person (including death), 
including, without limitation:
(a) claims for injuries or loss to employees of Transmission Owner, Eligible Customer,
Large Load Customer, or their respective Representatives, arising out of or relating to 
any act, omission, conduct, negligence or breach by Large Load Customer, or Eligible 
Customer, or their respective Representatives; 
(b) arising out of or relating to the work performed under or in connection with this CRA 
by Large Load Customer, Eligible Customer, or their respective Representatives;  and/or
(c) claims arising out of or relating to Large Load Customer’s, Eligible Customer’s, or 
their respective Representatives, contact or encounter with, compromise or disruption of, 
or loss or damage to any property of Transmission Owner or its Representatives’ 
property.  
Large Load Customer and Eligible Customer each specifically agree, to the fullest extent 
permitted by law, to assume liability for all personal injury claims brought by their respective 
employees or the employees of their respective Representatives in connection with this CRA and 
waive any limitation of liability defense based upon the applicable worker’s compensation law
and cases decided thereunder. Eligible Customer and Large Load Customer agree to indemnify 
and defend Transmission Owner and its Representatives from and against all such loss, expense 
damage or injury, including, without limitation, reasonable attorneys’ fees, which Transmission 
Owner’s Representatives may sustain as a result of personal injury claims by Eligible 
Customer’s or Large Load Customer’s employees or their respective Representatives’ employees 
or representatives.  
17.9
Enforcement Costs
Each of Eligible Customer and Large Load Customer shall reimburse Transmission Owner for 
all reasonable attorneys’ fees and expenses incurred in enforcing that Party’s obligations under 
this CRA, to the extent Transmission Owner prevails in the applicable action or proceeding. 
Notwithstanding the foregoing, Large Load Customer shall be solely responsible for all 
reasonable attorneys’ fees and expenses incurred by Transmission Owner in enforcing Large 
Load Customer’s payment or Credit Support obligations or collecting any amount due from 
Large Load Customer under this CRA.
Page 57

PDF page 106

17.10 Rules of Interpretation
The Parties intend that any grouping of words or phrases in a series of three or more shall be 
interpreted to include the use of the Oxford (or serial) comma regardless of whether such Oxford 
(or serial) comma is actually present in the series.
17.11 Survival
Expiration or termination of this CRA shall not affect:  (i) any payment obligation accrued or 
arising from a period before expiration or termination; (ii) any obligation to complete a 
calculation, invoice, reconciliation, refund, or return of an excess draw; (iii) any continuing right 
concerning Credit Support;  (iv) Large Load Customer’s obligation to pay any Shortfall Amount, 
Early Termination Amount, Partial Termination Amount, or other amount that survives 
expiration or termination; (v) Transmission Owner’s right to draw upon or apply Credit Support 
and its obligation to provide any required credit, refund, release, or reconciliation; (vi) the 
confidentiality and dispute-resolution provisions of Articles 14 and 15; or (vii) any other 
provision that expressly survives or that by its nature must survive to give effect to the Parties’ 
rights and obligations.
